        "C": "75",
        "D": "60"

      "correct_answer": "90",
      "correct_option_cracku": "A",
      "correct_option_cl": null,
      "correct_option_display": "A",
      "explanation": "Let A take <i>t</i> hours; B (a drain) takes <i>t − 1</i> hours to empty the full tank. Let the filling rate of C be <i>c</i> tanks/hour. The simultaneous fill condition gives <i>1/t − 1/(t − 1) + c = 1/2</i>. The staged condition (B and C for one hour, then C for 5/4 hours) gives <i>−1/(t − 1) + (9/4)c = 1</i>. Substituting for <i>c</i> yields <i>(t − 3)(t − 6) = 0</i>. The condition <i>t &lt; 5</i> selects <i>t = 3</i>. Then C fills at <i>2/3</i> tank/hour, so its time is <b>90 minutes</b>.",
